### T-106 — AutoLaris on the seller's key: instruction, status check, callback evidence
- Status: Done (2026-11-16; merged to `main` as `9602bfa` and `f6a1fb3` through pull request #10, CI green after one re-run of an unrelated T-123 failure; independent review (no blocker; 3 major, 9 minor, 4 nit) and confirmation review (2 minor, 3 nit) fixed; migration renumbered `0020` on top of T-105; track report `docs/tracks/t106-autolaris-seller-key.md`). Local only: fake AutoLaris accounts; the opt-in sandbox run needs owner approval
- Primary requirement: PR-5
- Constraints: ADR-0013 sections 3, 4, 5 and 6; D-21 (callback is evidence, verify by pull); T-49 contract kept where the ADR does not change it; official AutoLaris documentation is the contract (skill `autolaris-h2h`); paid status words, fee rates and expiry time zone are UNVERIFIED until confirmed with the provider; AutoLaris has no IP allow-list (owner statement, D-33), recorded in the provider-fact table and proven by the first sandbox call; rate limits RL-AUTOLARIS-CB, RL-PROVIDER-CHECK and RL-PAY-RECHECK from doc 16 (D-33)
- Dependencies: T-104
- Risk Level: R4
- Allowed Paths: `packages/db/src/payments/**`, `apps/web/src/app/(public-services)/api/payments/autolaris/**`, `apps/web/src/app/(seller)/seller/payments/**`, `apps/web/src/app/(checkout)/**` and `(public-services)/o/**` (method list, instruction and retry only), new migration, isolation registry, tests and E2E specs
- Done when: an order of a `seller_owned` store creates its instruction on that store's key; the callback route resolves the store from the signed reference and only stores evidence; the scheduled status check uses the owning store's key and settles only on the paid code plus an allow-listed status word, covering pending attempts and those expired in the last 24 hours, newest first. Seller settings exist for AutoLaris on/off, per-channel exclusion and `payment_fee_bearer`; Owner console holds the locked channels and the versioned fee rates. Master switch, channel exclusion and lock are checked at order submit. Retry keeps the old attempt; a late payment of it settles the order; a second settled attempt raises an anomaly (T-91). The seller can confirm a payment by hand with reason and evidence, one immutable record per attempt. A provider error leaves the order pending with a retry. Subscription invoices keep using the platform account. Tests: callback alone never settles; unknown status word never settles; store A's status check never uses store B's key; excluded or locked channel refused at submit; fee-bearer amounts frozen. Mutation checks: settle from the callback, accept an unlisted status word, and check with the platform key; a named test fails for each.
- Outcome (2026-11-16):
  - Built: migration `0020_t106_autolaris_seller_key.sql` (built as `0019`, regenerated as `0020` on top of T-105's `0019` at integration; all hand-written SQL in its `-- ===== T-106` section, the generated account FK line ends `NOT VALID`): attempt number, account snapshot, environment and frozen fee decision on `payment_attempt` (retry = next attempt, old one kept; insert guard enforces the retry rules), `payment_manual_confirmation` and `payment_anomaly` (FORCE RLS, append-only, retention classes with `row_filter false`), `payment_channel_lock`, versioned `payment_fee_rate` (version 1 = UNVERIFIED starting data), `payment_production_switch` (off), `payment_observation.status_word`, seller preferences on `store_payment_account`, `order_seller_payment_guard`, four-eyes row `providers.production_on`. Services in `payments/seller.ts` (fake per-key AutoLaris accounts, no live transport): one method function for list and submit, fee plan, buyer retry and check now (RL-PAY-RECHECK), seller manual confirmation (`finance.confirm_payment`, owner), anomalies, Owner console services only (locks, fee versions copied forward, sandbox pass, production ON with four-eyes; no screen), payment-ring re-seal job. Worker: instruction, verify and the scheduled `payment.status_check` (pending + expired < 24 h, newest first) on the owning store's key; callback stays evidence only; RL-AUTOLARIS-CB per reference 10 / 10 min. Screens: SC-32 preferences, new SC-33 `/seller/payments/transactions`, checkout channel list, confirmation and `/o/<token>` payment panel. Registry entries, platform column grants modelled (`platformColumns`), T-59 GRANT block regenerated.
  - Done-when: instruction on the store's key — "an order of a seller_owned store creates its instruction on that store's key …"; callback resolves the store and only stores evidence — "callback alone never settles …"; status check on the owning key, allow-listed word, window newest first — "unknown status word never settles …", "store A's status check never uses store B's key or the platform key", "the scheduled check covers pending attempts and those expired in the last 24 hours …"; seller settings, Owner console locks and fee versions, submit checks — "excluded or locked channel refused at submit …", "fee-bearer amounts frozen …", "Owner console payment settings need providers.manage …"; retry, late payment, anomaly — "retry keeps the old attempt …"; manual confirmation — "the seller confirms a payment by hand …"; provider error — "a provider error leaves the order pending …"; subscriptions on the platform account — "subscription invoices keep using the platform account …"; E2E `apps/web/e2e/t106.spec.ts` at mobile-320 and desktop. Table in the track report.

### T-108 — Affiliate membership per store and commission as a seller obligation
- Status: Planned
- Primary requirement: PR-28
- Constraints: ADR-0015 decisions 1 to 4; T-52 attribution rules unchanged; PRIV-2, PRIV-7; rate bounds 100 to 5000 bps and window 7 days, both Owner-configurable (D-31); RL-AFF-STORE-APPLY and RL-CLICK on the store host (doc 16, D-33)
- Dependencies: T-107
- Risk Level: R4
- Allowed Paths: `packages/db/src/affiliate/**`, `packages/db/src/seller/commerce/{checkout,orders}.ts` (attribution and status hooks only), `packages/db/src/ops/jobs/**` (one release job), new migration, isolation registry, `packages/db/drizzle-pending/t59-contract.sql`, tests
- Done when: `affiliate_membership` (store, affiliate, `pending` | `active` | `rejected` | `suspended`) exists under FORCE RLS; a store-level programme switch and default rate exist with per-product override. Attribution requires an `active` membership at order time. The commission deduction from the seller sale is removed, with the frozen base, rate and commission kept on the attribution. `affiliate_commission` holds one obligation per attributed order with the states of ADR-0015 decision 4; the order status writer voids on cancel or return before delivery; a scheduled job moves delivered orders to `payable` after `affiliate_hold_days`. Sandbox test orders earn nothing. Tests: one obligation per order under concurrent submits; no attribution without active membership; void and payable transitions at the exact boundary; store A cannot read or change store B's memberships or obligations; the seller's `order_financials` proceeds are identical with and without attribution. Migration proven on a populated copy of the dev database. Mutation checks: attribute without membership, release before delivery, and deduct commission from proceeds; a named test fails for each.

### T-109 — Affiliate payment records, shared statement and arrears
- Status: Planned
- Primary requirement: PR-28
- Constraints: ADR-0015 decisions 5 to 9; no provider call, no money movement; affiliate bank details shown only to the paying store's finance role (D-31)
- Dependencies: T-108
- Risk Level: R4
- Allowed Paths: `packages/db/src/affiliate/**`, `packages/db/src/ops/**` (arrears queue and freeze under `requirePlatformCap`), new migration, isolation registry, tests
- Done when: `affiliate_payment` records one seller payment to one affiliate (amount, date, method, typed bank transaction data per ADR-0015 decision 5, covered commissions; no file upload — proof upload belongs to a later complaint or ticket flow, not in this task) and is immutable; a correction is a reversing record. A payment's amount equals the sum of its covered commissions and recorded corrections, enforced in the database transaction. The affiliate can confirm or dispute. One statement query serves both the seller and the affiliate. A `payable` commission older than `affiliate_arrears_days` (default 14) is in arrears; the Owner console can freeze and unfreeze a store's programme, which stops new memberships and attributions and keeps existing obligations. Programme terms and the affiliate notice are stored with a version on acceptance. Tests: sum mismatch refused; double coverage of one commission refused under concurrency; edit and delete refused; seller and affiliate statements equal for the same data; arrears boundary; frozen store attributes nothing; no buyer contact data or order number in any affiliate projection. Mutation checks: allow a commission in two payments, allow an edit, and attribute while frozen; a named test fails for each.

### T-111 — Product kind and checkout rules for digital and mixed orders
- Status: Planned
- Primary requirement: PR-4
- Constraints: ADR-0016 sections 1 to 3, 5 and decisions 19 to 22; ADR-0002 (one store per order); ADR-0013 section 4 (one eligibility function); T-61 weight rule for physical lines; money stays integer IDR and server-derived
- Dependencies: T-105, T-106, T-107
- Risk Level: R4
- Allowed Paths: `packages/db/src/schema.ts` (`product`, `variant`, `order` columns and CHECKs), `packages/db/src/seller/commerce/{catalog,checkout,orders,dashboard}.ts`, `packages/db/src/storefront/{storefront,feeds,landing}.ts`, `packages/db/src/shipping/shipping.ts` (physical-lines weight only), `packages/db/src/promotions/**` (free-shipping threshold), `packages/db/src/ops/jobs/notify.ts`, new migration, isolation registry, `apps/web/src/app/(seller)/seller/products/**`, `apps/web/src/app/(storefront)/**`, `apps/web/src/app/(checkout)/**`, tests and E2E specs
- Done when: `product.kind` is `physical` | `digital`, changeable only while no order line refers to the product; a digital variant has no weight and unlimited stock unless the seller switches counting on; database CHECKs make weight mandatory for physical variants and shipping columns nullable only for orders without a physical line. Checkout quotes from physical lines only; a digital-only order skips address, courier and shipping fee and requires e-mail; a digital line removes COD from the method list and the server refuses it at submit. Free-shipping thresholds ignore digital lines. A digital-only order becomes `completed` when paid; a mixed order follows the parcel. Feeds exclude digital products. E-mails, order pages and the seller dashboard show no shipping block for an order without a parcel. Activation follows ADR-0016 decision 21. Tests: digital-only, mixed and physical-only orders through the real checkout functions; direct COD submit with a digital line refused; kind change refused after a sale; lifecycle rows of ADR-0016 section 5; existing physical suites unchanged. Migration proven on a populated copy of the dev database. Mutation checks: include a digital line in the shipping weight, allow COD with a digital line, and complete a mixed order at payment; a named test fails for each.

### T-112 — Access links, grants and digital delivery
- Status: Planned
- Primary requirement: PR-2
- Constraints: ADR-0016 section 4, section 7 and decisions 18, 20, 23, 24; ADR-0013 section 6 (settlement is the only trigger); ADR-0005 (RLS, isolation registry); SEC-3; the platform stores no file and the server never requests an access link; the member area, embedded video and webinars are out of scope (direction in D-31, not scheduled); caps 10 links per product and 2,000 characters per link, no default expiry (D-31); RL-ACCESS-OPEN (doc 16, D-33); the seller's acceptance of the content-rights terms is stored by T-116
- Dependencies: T-111
- Risk Level: R4
- Allowed Paths: a new `packages/db/src/seller/commerce/digital.ts` and its schema file, `packages/db/src/payments/**` (grant in the settle transaction only), `packages/db/src/seller/commerce/orders.ts` (revoke on cancel or refund), `packages/db/src/affiliate/**` (payable trigger for digital lines), `apps/web/src/app/(public-services)/o/**`, `apps/web/src/app/(seller)/seller/{products,orders}/**`, `packages/db/src/platform/host.ts` (reserved member paths), the reserved member-area folders, new migration, isolation registry, ARCH-5 rows, tests, E2E specs, `docs/evidence/**`
- Done when: `product_access_item` (type `link` only; a CHECK or enum that refuses the reserved types `video_embed` and `live_session` until their task exists) and `digital_grant` (one per digital order line; store, order line, buyer identity, state, optional expiry) exist store-scoped under FORCE RLS. The seller can add, order and remove access links and write an access note; `https` only, no credentials, length and count caps enforced on the server; the product screen states that sharing permissions stay on the seller's host. The grant is created in the transaction that settles the order (status check or manual confirmation) and nowhere else. One function resolves a grant to its content and is the only reader. The signed order-status page shows label, destination host and link for an active grant, opening in a new tab with no referrer; nothing is shown after expiry or revocation. The seller can extend and revoke a grant, audited. Cancel or refund revokes. The payment-confirmed e-mail carries the order-status link, never an access link. Per-product takedown is not built (C-G1): while a store is suspended its access links are not shown. The member-area structure of ADR-0016 item 27 exists: module and route folders without pages or handlers, their paths reserved in the host dispatch and answering `404` on every host, absent from navigation, sitemap, feeds and the API description; a test proves each reserved path is unreachable. Tests: no grant before paid and none from a callback alone; one grant per line under concurrent and replayed settlement; access links and note absent from storefront HTML, Storefront API, feeds, e-mails and logs; invalid links refused on save; reserved types refused; no outbound request to a link; expired and revoked grants show nothing; access note with markup rendered as text; store A cannot read or revoke store B's access items and grants. A redacted order has its grants revoked (C-G3). E2E at 320 px and desktop: digital-only purchase to the access link, mixed purchase, seller grant actions. Mutation checks: grant on order creation, show the link for a revoked grant, and include an access link in the product API response; a named test fails for each.

### T-113 — Bank transfer to the seller's own account
- Status: Done 2026-11-23: merged through pull request #19 (CI run 45116778288: DB 82 files / 1560 tests, E2E 299 passed / 6 skipped, image ok); migration `0023`, D-36; mutations, populated-copy proof and the independent review (all findings handled, confirmation APPROVE) in [`docs/tracks/t113-bank-transfer.md`](tracks/t113-bank-transfer.md)
- Primary requirement: PR-5
- Constraints: ADR-0013 section 10 and decisions 14, 24; D-31; ADR-0005 (RLS, isolation registry); no provider call, no bank statement check; the buyer's proof upload is private and never changes the order by itself; the existing `manual_transfer` value keeps its meaning (provider channel)
- Dependencies: T-104, T-107
- Risk Level: R4
- Allowed Paths: `packages/db/src/schema.ts` (`payment_method` value `bank_transfer`, order CHECKs), a new store bank-account table in the payments schema file, `packages/db/src/payments/**`, `packages/db/src/seller/commerce/{checkout,orders}.ts`, `packages/db/src/ops/jobs/notify.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/(seller)/seller/{payments,orders}/**`, `apps/web/src/app/(checkout)/**`, `apps/web/src/app/(public-services)/o/**` (proof upload and private download), `apps/web/src/lib/media.ts` (private store beside the public one), `packages/db/src/cleanup.ts` (proof retention), `.env.example` (private store path), new migration, isolation registry, tests and E2E specs, `docs/evidence/**`
- Done when: a store lists up to five bank accounts (bank, number, holder) with `integrations.manage` and a fresh step-up, audited, store-scoped under FORCE RLS. The method is offered only when one account is listed and the store has it switched on; the server refuses it at submit otherwise. Confirmation page, order-status page and order e-mail show the accounts and the exact total. Stock is reserved and the order expires after the payment window. The buyer can upload up to three proof images on the signed order-status page (type checked by content, size cap, rate limited); the files are stored outside the public media directory, readable only through that page and by the store's finance role, served as an attachment with the sandbox policy, and deleted by the cleanup job 90 days after the order closes (Owner-configurable setting, D-33); uploads are limited by RL-PROOF-UPLOAD (doc 16). The seller sees the proof on the order, confirms or rejects it with a reason; a rejected proof allows a new upload. Only the seller's manual confirmation (finance capability, reason, append-only record linking the proof) makes the order paid, and it triggers the same post-payment effects as a provider status check, including digital grants (T-112) once that task exists. Tests: method absent and refused without an account; a direct submit with the method on a store that has none is refused; no path other than the confirmation sets paid, including an upload; the public media route cannot serve a proof; store A's finance role and another buyer's token cannot read it; a non-image or oversized file is refused; retention deletes the file and keeps the confirmation record; a second confirmation changes nothing; expiry releases stock; store A cannot read or change store B's accounts. E2E at 320 px and desktop: order by bank transfer, seller confirms, buyer sees paid. Mutation checks: offer the method without an account, mark paid without a confirmation record, and serve a proof from the public route; a named test fails for each.

### T-116 — Seller terms, notices and privacy statements for the seller-owned model
- Status: Planned (added 2026-11-15 from the decision-coverage check; wording needs a privacy or legal adviser before production)
- Primary requirement: PR-27
- Constraints: ADR-0013 section 8 (financial role, shipping money) and its D-33 table; ADR-0015 decision 8; ADR-0016 decision 23; D-33 item 3 (working position, not a legal conclusion; Q-11 stays open); 13-COMPLIANCE-PRIVACY; no statement promises custody, guarantee or reconciliation that Bukalaris does not perform; texts are Indonesian UI copy, rendered as text
- Dependencies: T-104, T-107
- Risk Level: R3
- Allowed Paths: a seller-terms module and schema file under `packages/db/src/platform/` (versioned text and acceptance record), `apps/web/src/app/(seller)/seller/{settings,privacy,payments,shipping}/**` (notices only), `apps/web/src/app/(checkout)/**` and `(public-services)/o/**` (payee notice only), new migration, isolation registry, ARCH-5 rows, `docs/13-COMPLIANCE-PRIVACY.md`, `docs/17-UX-FLOWS-SCREEN-CONTRACTS.md`, tests and E2E specs, `docs/evidence/**`
- Done when: one versioned seller-terms text exists and a store's owner accepts it before a provider key or bank account can be saved; the acceptance stores version, user and time and is append-only. The text states: buyer payments and COD cash go to the seller's own accounts and never pass through Bukalaris; Bukalaris keeps a written record of platform transactions and reconciles no external account; shipping money is settled between the seller and Mengantar; the seller is responsible for its provider accounts and for buyer data held there; a redaction in Bukalaris does not reach a provider's copy; the seller holds the right to sell any digital content it lists. The seller privacy screen (SC-24) shows the provider-copy notice beside the redaction action. Checkout and the order-status page name the store as the party receiving the payment. A new terms version asks for acceptance again and blocks nothing already running. The adviser review is recorded (name, date, version) before the production switch of T-105 or T-106 is turned on; without it the texts stay marked as draft. Tests: saving a key or bank account without acceptance is refused on the server; store A's acceptance does not satisfy store B; an acceptance cannot be edited or deleted; the notices render at 320 px and desktop. Mutation check: allow a key save without acceptance; a named test fails.

### T-123 — Diagnose the intermittent no-JS add-to-cart timeout in `auth-csrf.spec.ts:123`
- Status: Partial (2026-11-16): five of six occurrences explained and fixed: `t68.spec.ts` submit race and `notify.test.ts` tamper case (pull request #4), the no-JS click hang (1), (4), (6) (pull request #12, merged as `97665d2`, CI green); the `content.spec.ts` settings save (3) remains unexplained
- Primary requirement: PR-14
- Risk Level: R2
- Allowed Paths: `apps/web/e2e/auth-csrf.spec.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/helpers.ts`; application files only for an evidenced root cause
- Finding: CI run 43740385003 attempt 1 on `main` (`2b4d0a4`): "T82 anonymous cart native forms reject forged tokens and accept the current cart binding" [desktop] hit the 90 s test timeout. The page snapshot shows the product page with the "Tambah ke keranjang" button: the JavaScript-free form submit never navigated. No server error was logged; 260 tests passed. The same test passed at mobile-320 in that run, in pull request #1 (run 43709386002), in two local full runs and in 8 local repetitions of the spec on desktop (88/88). The spec sets `trace: "off"` because it handles CSRF and session values, so no trace exists.
- Done when: the hang is reproduced or its cause is shown from evidence (for example a bounded per-step timeout with a server-side request log, or a trace mode that redacts bodies and headers); the root cause is fixed or, if it is in the test harness, the harness is corrected; no retry or flaky label without that evidence.
- Outcome 2026-11-16:
  - Same symptom seen three times: a form submit that never takes effect. (1) `auth-csrf.spec.ts:123` [desktop], CI run 43740385003 attempt 1 (above). (2) `t68.spec.ts:22` [desktop], CI run 43752508707 on `main` (`74d3e76`, a documentation-only commit): "triaged" never appeared. (3) `content.spec.ts:44` [desktop] in a local T-104 run: "Tersimpan." never appeared after the settings save; passed on re-run.
  - (2) explained from its CI trace: the "Simpan penugasan" server action POST was answered, the test then selected the status and pressed Enter on "Simpan status", and no second POST was ever sent. The test's check after the first submit (`toHaveValue` of the field it had just typed) passes at once, so nothing waited for the first action. Deterministic reproduction (temporary harness, not committed): hold the assignment response until "Simpan status" has focus, release it, wait for its `?saved=assignment` redirect, then press Enter: the test fails with the same "triaged" error. Fix in `apps/web/e2e/t68.spec.ts`: wait for `?saved=assignment` before the next form; with the assignment response delayed by 1.5 s the fixed test passes. This is a test-order race, not an application defect: a person cannot press a button whose page is being replaced.
  - (4) `auth-csrf.spec.ts:307` [mobile-320], CI run 43766765394 (this task's pull request): the no-JS "Tambah ke keranjang" click waited 90 s because Playwright saw the button as "element is not stable" on every attempt (its box kept moving), with JavaScript disabled. This is very likely what happened in (1) too: the page stayed on the product page because the click never ran. Unproven hypothesis: a layout oscillation on the product page (for example a scrollbar appearing and disappearing across a container-query breakpoint such as `@3xl:`), which depends on how much content earlier specs left on the seeded product. Next step: when the click is stuck, sample the button's box over a few frames and the document width and scrollbar state, and save a screenshot.
  - (5) `packages/db/test/notify.test.ts` "signed status link …", CI run 43769182429: the tamper case replaced the first character of the signature with "A" or "B" chosen by the LAST character, so whenever the first character already was that letter the signature stayed valid (about 1 run in 64). Fixed: the choice now looks at the first character.
  - (1) and (3) have no trace (`auth-csrf.spec.ts` turns traces off; (3) was a local run). Left open: next time, keep the trace of `content.spec.ts`, and give `auth-csrf.spec.ts` a body- and header-free diagnostic (request log of the click) before guessing.
- Outcome 2026-11-16 (second pass, branch `task/t123-nojs-submit`):
  - (6) `auth-csrf.spec.ts:307` [desktop], CI run 43818833234 attempt 1 (pull request #10): in the `add` helper (`javaScriptEnabled: false`), after `getByLabel("Jumlah").fill("7")`, the "Tambah ke keranjang" click logged `attempting click action`, `2 × waiting for element to be visible, enabled and stable` / `element is not stable`, `retrying click action`, `waiting 20ms`, and then nothing until the 90 s test timeout: only two stability checks in about 88 s, so the click was stuck inside Playwright's own retry, not waiting on the server.
  - Cause, proven: in the installed Playwright 1.63.0 (`playwright-core/lib/coreBundle.js`, `_retryAction`) a pointer action that finds its target "not stable" retries after waits of 0, 20, 100, 100, 500 ms, and each non-zero wait is a `setTimeout` evaluated in Playwright's isolated utility world of the page. With JavaScript disabled, Chromium never runs `setTimeout` callbacks there, while `requestAnimationFrame` callbacks (which the stability check itself uses) still run. Measured over CDP in an isolated world of a JS-disabled page: `setTimeout(20)` and `setTimeout(0)` never fired within 3 s, `requestAnimationFrame` fired; with JS enabled all three fired. So in a JS-disabled context any click whose target is "not stable" on the first two checks hangs for ever at "waiting 20ms". Reproduced: (a) a standalone page with a CSS-animated button gives exactly the CI call log with JS disabled and stops there, while with JS enabled the same click keeps retrying (20 ms, 100 ms, ... waits); (b) a temporary spec (not committed) on the real product page, the button made permanently unstable by a CSSOM-inserted animation, JS disabled: `click()` timed out at 10 s with the CI call log word for word (4/4, desktop and mobile-320), focus plus Enter added 7 to the cart in 112 to 158 ms (4/4).
  - Inferred, not proven: (1) and (4) are the same hang (same JS-disabled add-to-cart, same "element is not stable" in (4), the page stayed on the product page in (1)). What made the real button move for two checks in CI is unknown: no smooth scrolling exists in the app; the shadcn button has `transition-all`. It no longer matters for the suite, because the fixed path never waits for stability.
  - Fix (test harness; no application change): `apps/web/e2e/helpers.ts` `activateNoJs(target)` = `target.press("Enter")`. Per the installed `Locator.press` documentation it focuses the element and presses the key, with no actionability wait (the source path is `_press` → focus → keyboard, outside `_retryAction`); keyboard activation of a submit button submits its form with that button as the submitter (checked: the POST body carried the pressed button's `name=value`, not the other button's), and Enter on a focused link follows it. Every activation of a button or link in a `javaScriptEnabled: false` context now uses it: `auth-csrf.spec.ts` (contexts at lines 10, 55, 124 and the `add` helper at 314, including its first-product link), `host-mode-two-root.spec.ts` (context at 485) and `host-mode.spec.ts` (context at 451); `chooseDestination` takes an optional `activate` argument, passed only by those two no-JS host-mode tests. Clicks in JS-enabled contexts are unchanged (their retry timers fire). `t82-callers.spec.ts` (49, 113) and `host-mode.spec.ts` (393) needed no change: their no-JS pages only read forms; the posts go through `request`.
  - Deviation: `host-mode.spec.ts` and `host-mode-two-root.spec.ts` are outside this task's Allowed Paths; changed for the same root cause.
  - Verification (worktree, CI placeholder env from `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, own databases `bukalaris_t123b*`, `E2E_PORT=4200`, all under `heavy-run --lock bukalaris-env`): `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`, `pnpm lint` (exit 0), `pnpm typecheck` (exit 0), `pnpm build` (exit 0); `pnpm --filter @bukalaris/web e2e e2e/auth-csrf.spec.ts --project mobile-320 --project desktop --repeat-each 5`: 110 passed (11 tests x 2 projects x 5); `... e2e/host-mode.spec.ts e2e/host-mode-two-root.spec.ts --project host-mode --repeat-each 3`: 84 passed; the temporary reproduction spec above: 8 passed (4 hangs shown, 4 fixed-path submits), then deleted. No retry and no flaky label added.

### T-124 — Check whether `BETTER_AUTH_SECRETS` can override the per-surface auth secrets
- Status: Done (2026-11-16; merged to `main` through pull request #5, CI green): risk confirmed from the library source, guard added and proven by mutation
- Primary requirement: PR-14
- Risk Level: R3
- Allowed Paths: `apps/web/src/lib/auth.ts`, a test under `packages/db/test/` or `apps/web/e2e/`, `docs/DEPLOY-COOLIFY.md`, `docs/RUNBOOK.md`
- Finding: Better Auth may read `BETTER_AUTH_SECRETS` before the `secret` option that `apps/web/src/lib/auth.ts` sets per surface (T-70, D-21). If so, setting that variable in an environment would give every surface the same secret and undo the per-surface separation.
- Done when: a test sets `BETTER_AUTH_SECRETS` and shows whether two surfaces then share a secret; if they do, the app refuses to start with it set (or ignores it) and DEPLOY-COOLIFY and RUNBOOK say so; mutation check: remove the guard, a named test fails.
- Outcome 2026-11-16:
  - Confirmed in the installed Better Auth 1.7.7: `context/create-context.mjs` takes `options.secrets ?? parseSecretsEnv(env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRETS)` and, when present, signs and encrypts with its first entry; the per-surface `secret` option is then only the legacy fallback. With the variable set every surface would share one secret.
  - `apps/web/src/lib/auth.ts` `createAuth` refuses to build any surface while `BETTER_AUTH_SECRETS` is set (fail closed); `docs/DEPLOY-COOLIFY.md` says never to set it.
  - Test: `apps/web/e2e/host-mode-t124-secrets.spec.ts` starts a production server with the variable set (own port, `E2E_PORT + 5`): no surface (`owner`, `app`, `affiliate`, `buyer`) signs in or issues a session cookie. Mutation (guard removed, rebuilt): the test fails with status 200 instead of >= 500. With the guard: the spec and the host-mode project pass (17/17); lint and typecheck pass.
  - Deviation: the test is an E2E spec rather than a unit test, because `auth.ts` is a server-only module of the web app and only `packages/db` has unit tests.
### T-125 — Keep the E2E browser away from the real pixel provider hosts
- Status: Done (2026-11-16; merged to `main` as `cacb98d` and `cbe2379` through pull request #11, CI green)
- Primary requirement: PR-14
- Risk Level: R1
- Allowed Paths: `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/**`, `docs/TASKS.md`
- Finding: the T-106 integration run (2026-11-16) failed `[host-mode] host-mode-two-root.spec.ts:183` on a CSP violation `connect-src https://analytics-ipv6.tiktokw.us/ipv6/enrich_ipv6`. `signals.spec.ts` enables TikTok and Meta pixels on `toko-a` in the shared E2E database and fulfils the provider scripts with `page.route` only inside its own pages; every later spec that opens a `toko-a` page loaded the real provider scripts from the internet, whose beacons change without notice. The result depended on the network and on the providers, locally and in CI.
- Done when: the E2E browser cannot resolve the pixel provider hosts (a spec proves it); `signals.spec.ts` (route-fulfilled scripts) and the host-mode specs pass; no application code changes.
- Outcome 2026-11-16:
  - `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`: the E2E browser starts with `--host-resolver-rules` that map the pixel provider domains (and their subdomains) to "not found": `facebook.net`, `facebook.com`, `tiktok.com`, `tiktokw.us`, `googletagmanager.com`, `google.com`, `google.co.id`, `googleadservices.com`, `doubleclick.net`, `google-analytics.com`. `page.route` runs before name resolution, so `signals.spec.ts` keeps its fulfilled scripts.
  - Test `apps/web/e2e/t125-third-party.spec.ts`: a page fetch to four provider URLs (including the TikTok IPv6 beacon that broke the run) fails, and a route-fulfilled script still loads.
  - Commands (CI-style placeholder env, databases `bukalaris_t125*`, port 4100): `pnpm build`, then `playwright test t125-third-party.spec.ts signals.spec.ts host-mode-two-root.spec.ts security-headers.spec.ts`: 25 passed, 1 skipped. Lint and typecheck pass.
  - Mutation: launch arguments emptied (`args: [] || [...]`): the first T-125 test failed (`https://analytics.tiktok.com/i18n/pixel/events.js` reached); config restored, sha256 matched.
  - CI run 43819879980 (pull request #11) then failed `[desktop] catalog.spec.ts:6`: it asserts no console errors, and with the hosts unresolvable the pixel scripts that `signals.spec.ts` [mobile-320] had switched on for `toko-a` failed to load (`net::ERR_NAME_NOT_RESOLVED`, 6 entries). Root cause, not the resolver rule: `signals.spec.ts` left the pixels on for every later spec. Fix: `signals.spec.ts` `afterAll` switches `toko-a`'s `signal_destination` rows off again (E2E database only); the resolver rule stays as the guard against real network access.
  - After the fix: `playwright test catalog.spec.ts signals.spec.ts t125-third-party.spec.ts host-mode-two-root.spec.ts`: 24 passed (desktop `catalog.spec.ts` runs after mobile-320 `signals.spec.ts`, the CI order). Mutation: `afterAll` disabled: `[desktop] catalog.spec.ts:6` failed with the same `ERR_NAME_NOT_RESOLVED` entries; file restored, sha256 matched.

### T-126 — Pin the AES-GCM tag length and remove the `ORDER_LINK_SECRET` fallback (review 2026-11-23)
- Status: Done 2026-11-23: merged through pull request #16 (CI run 45055233196: DB 79 files / 1511 tests, E2E 291 passed / 4 skipped, image ok; the first run failed `t65-literal-scan.test.ts` on the new `16` in `tax/seal.ts`, now on its allow-list as the GCM tag size)
- Primary requirement: PR-14 (SEC-11, SEC-22)
- Risk Level: R2
- Allowed Paths: `packages/db/src/storefront/signals.ts`, `packages/db/src/tax/seal.ts`, `packages/db/src/ops/jobs/notify.ts`, `packages/db/src/worker.ts`, `apps/web/src/instrumentation.ts`, the tests and test configuration that set the variable (`packages/db/vitest.config.ts`, `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/**`, `packages/db/test/**`, `scripts/verify-docker.sh`), `.env.example`, `docs/DEPLOY-COOLIFY.md`, `docs/RUNBOOK.md` (including the image smoke test by hand), `docs/04-SYSTEM-ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/TASKS.md`, `BUILD-LOG.md`
- Finding (repository review 2026-11-23): `openToken` (`signals.ts`) and `unsealTaxValue` (`tax/seal.ts`) did not pin the GCM tag length, and Node 24 accepts a truncated tag (DEP0182 warning only); `shipping/credentials.ts` already checks 16 bytes. `ORDER_LINK_SECRET` fell back to `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` (`notify.ts`), against SEC-22 ("no secret silently falls back to another").
- Done when: a token or tax value with a 4-byte prefix of its real tag is refused; without `ORDER_LINK_SECRET` no link is signed, the worker exits before claiming and names the variable, and a production web server answers no request; each guard removed once makes a named check fail.
- Outcome 2026-11-23:
  - `createDecipheriv(..., { authTagLength: 16 })` in both places. `notify.ts` exports `requireOrderLinkSecret()` without fallback; `worker.ts` calls it after the mail check; `instrumentation.ts` `register` throws in production without the variable (Next 16.4 then answers every request with 500, the process stays up).
  - Tests: `signals.test.ts` "token sealing (SEC-11)" and `t65-tax.test.ts` "a sealed value is bound …" (truncated tag); `notify.test.ts` two SEC-22 tests (no fallback; worker exits non-zero naming the variable); `apps/web/e2e/host-mode-t126-order-link.spec.ts` (production server on `E2E_PORT + 6` without the variable: `/api/health` and `/login` 500). Placeholders added to `vitest.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts` and both containers of `verify-docker.sh`; the two specs that signed links with the fallback now use `ORDER_LINK_SECRET`.
  - Executed locally (this device has no Docker, so no database): the same assertions as a Node script on the real modules: 4-byte tag refused (signals and tax), unset secret refused with `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` set; worker run with `NODE_ENV=production`, `MAIL_MODE=local`, no `ORDER_LINK_SECRET`: exit 1, "ORDER_LINK_SECRET is not set"; `pnpm build`, then `server.js` without the variable: `/api/health` 500, with it: 200.
  - Mutations (same script; files restored, `sha256sum -c` OK): `authTagLength` removed → the 4-byte tag opened both values; fallback restored → a link was signed and the worker passed the check to the database step.
  - Independent review after the merge (2026-11-23, Antigravity CLI `agy` via `ai-ask`, read-only, diff and file excerpts attached; Codex and Claude were unavailable: 401 and weekly limit): APPROVE, no weakened check. Low 1 (the "no link is verified" test never called `verifyOrderLink`): fixed in the Records pull request, a link signed with `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` now makes `verifyOrderLink` throw. Low 2 (a whitespace-only value passes `!s`): left to the SEC-22 start-up check below.
